# ORAG v0.1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Ship ORAG v0.1.0: a single offline `orag` executable that ingests UTF-8 text/Markdown into collections, answers questions with hybrid (BM25 + dense) retrieval and a local LLM, streams grounded answers with validated citations and a retrieval trace, and includes the evaluation harness that will pick the default models.

**Architecture:** One Rust package (`crates/orag`, library + binary) inside a Cargo workspace. Pure domain logic (`domain`) has no I/O; `store` owns SQLite (FTS5 + sqlite-vec `=0.1.9`), `infer` owns llama.cpp through `llama-cpp-2` behind `Embedder`/`Generator` traits, `ingest` runs a durable job worker, `retrieval` fuses lexical and dense hits with RRF and builds the cited answer, `server` exposes the authenticated loopback HTTP API (axum), `eval` measures retrieval quality and vector-search latency.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 1.98.1 (edition 2024), tokio 1.53, axum 0.8.9, rusqlite 0.40 (bundled SQLite), sqlite-vec 0.1.9, llama-cpp-2 0.1.158, pulldown-cmark 0.13, clap 4.6, serde/serde_json, thiserror 2, tracing.

**Spec:** `.docs/orag-decisions.md` (decisions D-001…D-018, binding) and `.docs/orag-architecture-design-notes.md` (long-term vision). Read both before Task 1.

---

## Global Constraints

- Language: Rust `1.98.1` pinned in `rust-toolchain.toml`; edition 2024; `rust-version = "1.90"`.
- Exactly one ML runtime: `llama-cpp-2 = "=0.1.158"`. Do not add mistral.rs, candle, ort, fastembed (D-002).
- sqlite-vec is pinned `=0.1.9`. Never `^0.1.9` — it resolves to the 0.1.10 alpha series (D-003).
- Bundled SQLite must report version ≥ 3.51.3 (D-004).
- No implicit network access anywhere in the binary: no telemetry, no downloads, no cloud fallback (D-012).
- Bind only loopback addresses; every `/v1/*` route except `GET /v1/health` requires the bearer token (D-013).
- Collection id is a mandatory filter inside lexical and dense search, applied before top-k (D-014).
- Retrieval in v0.1 is deterministic hybrid: BM25 top 50 + dense top 50 → RRF k = 60 → at most 8 context chunks; no reranker, no rewriting (D-005).
- Lexical normalization: NFKC, fold `İ`/`I`/`ı` → `i`, lowercase, `unicode61 remove_diacritics 0` (D-006).
- Ingestion is asynchronous; publishing a document is one SQLite transaction (D-008).
- Embedding spaces are never mixed; fingerprint mismatch → `409 reindex_required` (D-009).
- Accepted formats in v0.1: `.txt` / `text/plain`, `.md` / `.markdown` / `text/markdown`, UTF-8 only (D-010).
- Versioning: every task ends by bumping the version with `scripts/bump-version.sh` and committing; the version listed in the task's last step is the exact value to use (D-017).
- Library code returns `orag::error::Result<T>`; `anyhow` only in `cli.rs`/`main.rs`. No `unwrap()`/`expect()` outside tests except on invariants documented by a comment.
- Code comments and docs in English. Files under 400 lines where practical, 800 hard maximum; functions under 50 lines.
- `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` and `cargo test` must pass before every commit.

## Review Focus

These are the inputs the spec implies but most likely to bite a real user. Each one has a pinned test in the owning task.

1. **A user query containing FTS5 syntax** (`"`, `*`, `NEAR(`, `col:`, `OR`, `-`) must never error or change semantics. It is searched as plain words. Tests: Task 3 (`fts_query_neutralizes_syntax`) and Task 10 (`lexical_search_survives_hostile_query`).
2. **A non-UTF-8 upload labeled as text** (a UTF-16 file from Windows, a binary renamed `.txt`) must be rejected with an actionable message, not indexed as garbage. Tests: Task 4 (`rejects_utf16_with_actionable_message`, `rejects_binary_nul_bytes`).
3. **A restart in the middle of ingestion** must resume the job without duplicate chunks. Test: Task 14 (`restart_requeues_running_job_and_indexes_once`).
4. **Deleting a document or collection while its job is running** must leave no orphan chunks, FTS rows or vectors. Tests: Task 10 (`delete_document_removes_fts_and_vectors`) and Task 14 (`publish_after_delete_is_discarded`).
5. **A client disconnecting mid-stream** must stop generation and release the generation permit. Test: Task 18 (`dropping_sse_stream_stops_generation`).

## Shared Procedures

### P1 — Version bump and commit (last step of every task)

Every task ends with this procedure. The task names the exact version and changelog bullet:

```bash
cargo fmt --all --check
cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings
cargo test
scripts/bump-version.sh <VERSION> "<CHANGELOG BULLET>"
cargo test version::tests        # CHANGELOG top entry == crate version
git add <the exact files listed in the task> Cargo.toml Cargo.lock CHANGELOG.md
git commit -m "<type>: <subject>" -m "Version <VERSION>."
```

Work happens on the branch `feat/v0.1` (create it in Task 1). Push after each task with `git push -u origin feat/v0.1`. Only this repository may be committed to or pushed from. Never tag. Opening the PR and merging are left to the owner.

### P2 — Running llama-backed tests

The `llama` feature compiles llama.cpp with CMake, which takes a few minutes on a cold build. For fast inner loops use `cargo test --no-default-features`. CI always runs with default features (plus `metal` on macOS). Real-model tests run only through `scripts/release-model-check.sh`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement**

`crates/orag/build.rs`:

```rust
use std::process::Command;

fn main() {
    let sha = Command::new("git")
        .args(["rev-parse", "--short=12", "HEAD"])
        .output()
        .ok()
        .filter(|out| out.status.success())
        .map(|out| String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string())
        .filter(|sha| !sha.is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());
    println!("cargo:rustc-env=ORAG_GIT_SHA={sha}");
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=../../.git/HEAD");
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=../../.git/refs");
}
```

`crates/orag/src/version.rs`:

```rust
//! Build and API version information.

use serde::Serialize;

/// Crate version; inherited from `[workspace.package] version`.
pub const VERSION: &str = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");
/// Short git commit of the build, or `unknown` outside a git checkout.
pub const GIT_SHA: &str = env!("ORAG_GIT_SHA");
/// HTTP contract version; changes only on breaking API changes.
pub const API_VERSION: &str = "v1";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct VersionInfo {
    pub version: &'static str,
    pub api: &'static str,
    pub schema_version: Option<u32>,
    pub git_sha: &'static str,
}

pub fn version_info(schema_version: Option<u32>) -> VersionInfo {
    VersionInfo {
        version: VERSION,
        api: API_VERSION,
        schema_version,
        git_sha: GIT_SHA,
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn changelog_top_entry_matches_crate_version() {
        let changelog = include_str!("../../../CHANGELOG.md");
        let top = changelog
            .lines()
            .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("## ["))
            .and_then(|rest| rest.split(']').next())
            .expect("CHANGELOG.md has a version heading");
        assert_eq!(top, VERSION, "bump with scripts/bump-version.sh");
    }
}
```

`crates/orag/src/error.rs`:

```rust
//! Library error type. Variants map to HTTP status codes in `server::errors`.

use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Debug, Error)]
pub enum OragError {
    #[error("{kind} {id} not found")]
    NotFound { kind: &'static str, id: i64 },
    #[error("conflict: {0}")]
    Conflict(String),
    #[error(
        "collection {collection_id} was indexed with a different embedding model; reindex required"
    )]
    ReindexRequired { collection_id: i64 },
    #[error("unsupported format: {0}")]
    UnsupportedFormat(String),
    #[error("invalid input: {0}")]
    InvalidInput(String),
    #[error("model error: {0}")]
    Model(String),
    #[error(
        "database schema version {found} is newer than this build supports ({supported}); upgrade orag"
    )]
    SchemaTooNew { found: u32, supported: u32 },
    #[error("io error: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
    #[error("internal error: {0}")]
    Internal(String),
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, OragError>;
```

`crates/orag/src/cli.rs`:

```rust
//! Command-line entry point.

use std::process::ExitCode;

use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};

use crate::version;

#[derive(Debug, Parser)]
#[command(name = "orag", version = version::VERSION, about = "Local-first, offline RAG engine")]
pub struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    pub command: Command,
}

#[derive(Debug, Subcommand)]
pub enum Command {
    /// Print version information as JSON.
    Version,
}

pub fn run() -> ExitCode {
    match execute(Cli::parse()) {
        Ok(()) => ExitCode::SUCCESS,
        Err(err) => {
            eprintln!("error: {err:#}");
            ExitCode::FAILURE
        }
    }
}

fn execute(cli: Cli) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    match cli.command {
        Command::Version => {
            let info = version::version_info(None);
            println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&info)?);
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Version scripts**

`scripts/bump-version.sh` (make executable with `chmod +x`):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Bumps the workspace version and prepends a CHANGELOG entry.
# Usage: scripts/bump-version.sh <new-version> <bullet> [<bullet>...]
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

new="${1:-}"
shift || true
if [[ ! "$new" =~ ^[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(-(alpha|beta|rc)\.[0-9]+)?$ ]]; then
  echo "invalid version: '$new'" >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [ "$#" -eq 0 ]; then
  echo "at least one changelog bullet is required" >&2
  exit 1
fi

current=$(sed -n 's/^version = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' Cargo.toml | head -1)
if [ "$current" = "$new" ]; then
  echo "version is already $new" >&2
  exit 1
fi

sed -i.bak "s/^version = \"$current\"$/version = \"$new\"/" Cargo.toml
rm -f Cargo.toml.bak

entry=$(mktemp)
{
  printf '## [%s] - %s\n\n' "$new" "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%d)"
  for bullet in "$@"; do printf -- '- %s\n' "$bullet"; done
  printf '\n'
} > "$entry"
awk -v f="$entry" '!done && /^## \[/ { while ((getline line < f) > 0) print line; done = 1 } { print }' \
  CHANGELOG.md > CHANGELOG.md.tmp
mv CHANGELOG.md.tmp CHANGELOG.md
rm -f "$entry"

cargo update --workspace --offline >/dev/null 2>&1 || cargo update --workspace >/dev/null
echo "bumped $current -> $new"
```

`scripts/check-version.sh` (executable):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# CI guard: Cargo version == top CHANGELOG entry (== git tag on tag builds).
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

cargo_version=$(sed -n 's/^version = "\(.*\)"$/\1/p' Cargo.toml | head -1)
changelog_version=$(sed -n 's/^## \[\([^]]*\)\].*/\1/p' CHANGELOG.md | head -1)

if [ "$cargo_version" != "$changelog_version" ]; then
  echo "Cargo.toml ($cargo_version) != CHANGELOG.md ($changelog_version)" >&2
  exit 1
fi
if [ "${GITHUB_REF_TYPE:-}" = "tag" ] && [ "${GITHUB_REF_NAME:-}" != "v$cargo_version" ]; then
  echo "tag ${GITHUB_REF_NAME} != v$cargo_version" >&2
  exit 1
fi
echo "version $cargo_version consistent"
```

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::collections::HashMap;

    fn env_of(pairs: &[(&str, &str)]) -> impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String> {
        let map: HashMap<String, String> =
            pairs.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.to_string())).collect();
        move |key| map.get(key).cloned()
    }

    #[test]
    fn defaults_apply_without_config_file() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let env = env_of(&[("ORAG_HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap())]);
        let cfg = Config::load(&env).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cfg.bind, "127.0.0.1:7613".parse().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(cfg.embedding_model, DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_MODEL);
        assert_eq!(cfg.generation_model, DEFAULT_GENERATION_MODEL);
        assert_eq!(cfg.max_upload_bytes, 32 * 1024 * 1024);
        assert_eq!(cfg.db_path(), home.path().join("orag.db"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn file_values_are_overridden_by_env() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        std::fs::write(
            home.path().join("config.toml"),
            "bind = \"127.0.0.1:9000\"\ngeneration_model = \"from-file\"\nmax_upload_mib = 4\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let env = env_of(&[
            ("ORAG_HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap()),
            ("ORAG_BIND", "127.0.0.1:9100"),
        ]);
        let cfg = Config::load(&env).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cfg.bind.port(), 9100);
        assert_eq!(cfg.generation_model, "from-file");
        assert_eq!(cfg.max_upload_bytes, 4 * 1024 * 1024);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_config_keys_are_rejected() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        std::fs::write(home.path().join("config.toml"), "bnd = \"x\"\n").unwrap();
        let env = env_of(&[("ORAG_HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap())]);
        assert!(matches!(Config::load(&env), Err(OragError::InvalidInput(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_loopback_bind_is_rejected() {
        assert!(parse_loopback_bind("0.0.0.0:7613").is_err());
        assert!(parse_loopback_bind("192.168.1.5:7613").is_err());
        assert!(parse_loopback_bind("[::1]:7613").is_ok());
        assert!(parse_loopback_bind("localhost:7613").is_err()); // names are not resolved
    }

    #[test]
    fn api_token_is_created_once_with_private_permissions() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("api-token");
        let first = ensure_api_token(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(first.len(), 64);
        assert!(first.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
        assert_eq!(ensure_api_token(&path).unwrap(), first);
        #[cfg(unix)]
        {
            use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
            let mode = std::fs::metadata(&path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777;
            assert_eq!(mode, 0o600);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_api_token_is_an_actionable_error() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("api-token");
        std::fs::write(&path, "short").unwrap();
        let err = ensure_api_token(&path).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err.contains("delete it"), "{err}");
    }
}

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `config.rs`**

```rust
//! Runtime configuration: `$ORAG_HOME/config.toml` plus environment overrides.

use std::net::SocketAddr;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use serde::Deserialize;

use crate::error::{OragError, Result};

pub const DEFAULT_PORT: u16 = 7613;
pub const DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_MODEL: &str = "qwen3-embedding-0.6b-q8_0";
pub const DEFAULT_GENERATION_MODEL: &str = "qwen3.5-4b-q4_k_m";
const DEFAULT_MAX_UPLOAD_MIB: u32 = 32;
const MAX_UPLOAD_MIB_LIMIT: u32 = 1024;

#[derive(Debug, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct FileConfig {
    bind: Option<String>,
    embedding_model: Option<String>,
    generation_model: Option<String>,
    max_upload_mib: Option<u32>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Config {
    pub home: PathBuf,
    pub bind: SocketAddr,
    pub embedding_model: String,
    pub generation_model: String,
    pub max_upload_bytes: usize,
}

impl Config {
    /// Loads `$ORAG_HOME/config.toml` (optional) and applies `ORAG_BIND`,
    /// `ORAG_EMBEDDING_MODEL` and `ORAG_GENERATION_MODEL` overrides.
    pub fn load(env: &dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Result<Config> {
        let home = resolve_home(env)?;
        let file = read_file_config(&home.join("config.toml"))?;
        let bind_text = env("ORAG_BIND")
            .or(file.bind)
            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("127.0.0.1:{DEFAULT_PORT}"));
        let max_upload_mib = file.max_upload_mib.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_MAX_UPLOAD_MIB);
        if !(1..=MAX_UPLOAD_MIB_LIMIT).contains(&max_upload_mib) {
            return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
                "max_upload_mib must be between 1 and {MAX_UPLOAD_MIB_LIMIT}"
            )));
        }
        Ok(Config {
            bind: parse_loopback_bind(&bind_text)?,
            embedding_model: env("ORAG_EMBEDDING_MODEL")
                .or(file.embedding_model)
                .unwrap_or_else(|| DEFAULT_EMBEDDING_MODEL.to_string()),
            generation_model: env("ORAG_GENERATION_MODEL")
                .or(file.generation_model)
                .unwrap_or_else(|| DEFAULT_GENERATION_MODEL.to_string()),
            max_upload_bytes: max_upload_mib as usize * 1024 * 1024,
            home,
        })
    }

    pub fn db_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.home.join("orag.db")
    }

    pub fn models_dir(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.home.join("models")
    }

    pub fn token_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.home.join("api-token")
    }

    pub fn ensure_dirs(&self) -> Result<()> {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(self.models_dir())?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn resolve_home(env: &dyn Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    match env("ORAG_HOME") {
        Some(home) if !home.trim().is_empty() => Ok(PathBuf::from(home)),
        _ => dirs::home_dir()
            .map(|home| home.join(".orag"))
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                OragError::InvalidInput("cannot determine home directory; set ORAG_HOME".into())
            }),
    }
}

fn read_file_config(path: &Path) -> Result<FileConfig> {
    match std::fs::read_to_string(path) {
        Ok(text) => toml::from_str(&text)
            .map_err(|err| OragError::InvalidInput(format!("{}: {err}", path.display()))),
        Err(err) if err.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(FileConfig::default()),
        Err(err) => Err(err.into()),
    }
}

/// Parses `IP:PORT` and requires a loopback address (D-013). Host names are not resolved.
pub fn parse_loopback_bind(text: &str) -> Result<SocketAddr> {
    let addr: SocketAddr = text.parse().map_err(|_| {
        OragError::InvalidInput(format!("bind address `{text}` is not IP:PORT"))
    })?;
    if !addr.ip().is_loopback() {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
            "bind address {addr} is not loopback; v0.1 only serves 127.0.0.1 or ::1"
        )));
    }
    Ok(addr)
}

/// Returns the API token, creating a random 256-bit token (mode 0600) on first use.
pub fn ensure_api_token(path: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    match std::fs::read_to_string(path) {
        Ok(text) => {
            let token = text.trim();
            if token.len() == 64 && token.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
                Ok(token.to_string())
            } else {
                Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
                    "{} is malformed; delete it to generate a new token",
                    path.display()
                )))
            }
        }
        Err(err) if err.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
            getrandom::fill(&mut bytes)
                .map_err(|err| OragError::Internal(format!("random source unavailable: {err}")))?;
            let token = hex::encode(bytes);
            write_private_file(path, token.as_bytes())?;
            Ok(token)
        }
        Err(err) => Err(err.into()),
    }
}

fn write_private_file(path: &Path, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut options = std::fs::OpenOptions::new();
    options.write(true).create_new(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        options.mode(0o600);
    }
    let mut file = options.open(path)?;
    file.write_all(contents)?;
    file.sync_all()?;
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
//! Lexical normalization shared by indexing and querying (D-006).
//!
//! The same function must be applied to indexed text and to queries; changing
//! it requires bumping `NORMALIZER_VERSION`, which changes the embedding-space
//! fingerprint and forces a reindex.

use unicode_normalization::UnicodeNormalization;

pub const NORMALIZER_VERSION: u32 = 1;
const MAX_QUERY_TERMS: usize = 32;

/// NFKC, then fold `İ`/`I`/`ı` to `i`, drop U+0307, and lowercase everything else.
pub fn normalize_for_lexical(input: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(input.len());
    for ch in input.nfkc() {
        match ch {
            'İ' | 'I' | 'ı' => out.push('i'),
            '\u{0307}' => {}
            other => out.extend(other.to_lowercase()),
        }
    }
    out
}

/// Normalized, de-duplicated alphanumeric terms in first-seen order (max 32).
pub fn lexical_terms(input: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    let normalized = normalize_for_lexical(input);
    let mut terms: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    for term in normalized
        .split(|c: char| !c.is_alphanumeric())
        .filter(|term| !term.is_empty())
    {
        if !terms.iter().any(|existing| existing == term) {
            terms.push(term.to_string());
        }
        if terms.len() == MAX_QUERY_TERMS {
            break;
        }
    }
    terms
}

/// FTS5 MATCH expression with every term quoted and OR-ed. Terms contain only
/// alphanumeric characters, so no user input can reach FTS5 syntax.
pub fn fts_query(input: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let terms = lexical_terms(input);
    if terms.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let quoted: Vec<String> = terms.iter().map(|term| format!("\"{term}\"")).collect();
    Some(quoted.join(" OR "))
}
```

//! Minimal normalized document representation ("AST-lite") for v0.1.
//! Richer nodes (pages, figures, formulas, spans) arrive with PDF in v0.2.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Block {
    Heading { level: u8, text: String },
    Paragraph(String),
    ListItem(String),
    Code(String),
    /// Rows rendered as `cell | cell`, one row per line, header first.
    Table(String),
}

impl Block {
    pub fn text(&self) -> &str {
        match self {
            Block::Heading { text, .. } => text,
            Block::Paragraph(text) | Block::ListItem(text) | Block::Code(text) | Block::Table(text) => text,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ParsedDocument {
    pub title: Option<String>,
    pub blocks: Vec<Block>,
}

```rust
//! Source format detection from file name and content type.

use std::path::Path;

use crate::error::{OragError, Result};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SourceFormat {
    PlainText,
    Markdown,
}

impl SourceFormat {
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            SourceFormat::PlainText => "text",
            SourceFormat::Markdown => "markdown",
        }
    }

    /// Parses the stored/JSON name produced by `as_str`.
    pub fn from_name(name: &str) -> Result<SourceFormat> {
        match name {
            "text" => Ok(SourceFormat::PlainText),
            "markdown" => Ok(SourceFormat::Markdown),
            other => Err(OragError::UnsupportedFormat(format!(
                "format `{other}` (supported: text, markdown)"
            ))),
        }
    }

    pub fn detect(filename: Option<&str>, content_type: Option<&str>) -> Result<SourceFormat> {
        let mime = content_type
            .map(|ct| ct.split(';').next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_ascii_lowercase());
        let ext = filename
            .and_then(|name| Path::new(name).extension())
            .and_then(|ext| ext.to_str())
            .map(str::to_ascii_lowercase);
        match (mime.as_deref(), ext.as_deref()) {
            (Some("text/markdown"), _) | (_, Some("md" | "markdown")) => Ok(SourceFormat::Markdown),
            (Some("text/plain"), _) | (_, Some("txt")) => Ok(SourceFormat::PlainText),
            (Some("application/pdf"), _) | (_, Some("pdf")) => Err(OragError::UnsupportedFormat(
                "PDF support is planned for v0.2".into(),
            )),
            (mime, ext) => Err(OragError::UnsupportedFormat(format!(
                "content type `{}`, extension `{}` (supported: .txt, .md)",
                mime.unwrap_or("none"),
                ext.unwrap_or("none")
            ))),
        }
    }
}
```

`ingest/parse.rs`:

```rust
//! UTF-8 decoding and parsers producing `ParsedDocument`.

use pulldown_cmark::{Event, HeadingLevel, Options, Parser, Tag, TagEnd};

use crate::domain::document::{Block, ParsedDocument};
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};
use crate::ingest::format::SourceFormat;

/// Decodes UTF-8 (optional BOM). Rejects UTF-16 and binary content with actionable messages.
pub fn decode_utf8(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<&str> {
    if bytes.starts_with(&[0xFF, 0xFE]) || bytes.starts_with(&[0xFE, 0xFF]) {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(
            "UTF-16 text is not supported; save the file as UTF-8".into(),
        ));
    }
    let bytes = bytes.strip_prefix(&[0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF]).unwrap_or(bytes);
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|err| {
        OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
            "file is not valid UTF-8 (first invalid byte at offset {})",
            err.valid_up_to()
        ))
    })?;
    if text.contains('\0') {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput("file contains NUL bytes; it looks binary".into()));
    }
    Ok(text)
}

pub fn parse_document(format: SourceFormat, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<ParsedDocument> {
    let text = decode_utf8(bytes)?.replace("\r\n", "\n");
    Ok(match format {
        SourceFormat::PlainText => parse_plain(&text),
        SourceFormat::Markdown => parse_markdown(&text),
    })
}

fn parse_plain(text: &str) -> ParsedDocument {
    let mut blocks = Vec::new();
    let mut lines: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
    for line in text.lines() {
        if line.trim().is_empty() {
            flush_paragraph(&mut lines, &mut blocks);
        } else {
            lines.push(line.trim());
        }
    }
    flush_paragraph(&mut lines, &mut blocks);
    ParsedDocument { title: None, blocks }
}

fn flush_paragraph(lines: &mut Vec<&str>, blocks: &mut Vec<Block>) {
    if !lines.is_empty() {
        blocks.push(Block::Paragraph(lines.join(" ")));
        lines.clear();
    }
}

fn parse_markdown(text: &str) -> ParsedDocument {
    let mut state = MarkdownState::default();
    for event in Parser::new_ext(text, Options::ENABLE_TABLES) {
        state.handle(event);
    }
    state.finish()
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct MarkdownState {
    blocks: Vec<Block>,
    buf: String,
    item_depth: usize,
    in_code: bool,
    row: Vec<String>,
    rows: Vec<String>,
}

impl MarkdownState {
    fn handle(&mut self, event: Event<'_>) {
        match event {
            Event::Start(Tag::Heading { .. }) => self.flush_item_text(),
            Event::End(TagEnd::Heading(level)) => {
                let text = self.take();
                self.push_nonempty(text, |text| Block::Heading { level: heading_level(level), text });
            }
            Event::Start(Tag::Item) => {
                self.flush_item_text();
                self.item_depth += 1;
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::Item) => {
                self.flush_item_text();
                self.item_depth = self.item_depth.saturating_sub(1);
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::Paragraph) => {
                if self.item_depth > 0 {
                    self.buf.push(' ');
                } else {
                    let text = self.take();
                    self.push_nonempty(text, Block::Paragraph);
                }
            }
            Event::Start(Tag::CodeBlock(_)) => {
                self.flush_item_text();
                self.in_code = true;
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::CodeBlock) => {
                self.in_code = false;
                let code = std::mem::take(&mut self.buf).trim_end().to_string();
                self.push_nonempty(code, Block::Code);
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::TableCell) => {
                let cell = self.take();
                self.row.push(cell);
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::TableHead | TagEnd::TableRow) => {
                let row = std::mem::take(&mut self.row);
                self.rows.push(row.join(" | "));
            }
            Event::End(TagEnd::Table) => {
                let rows = std::mem::take(&mut self.rows);
                self.push_nonempty(rows.join("\n"), Block::Table);
            }
            Event::Text(text) | Event::Code(text) => self.buf.push_str(&text),
            Event::SoftBreak => self.buf.push(if self.in_code { '\n' } else { ' ' }),
            Event::HardBreak => self.buf.push('\n'),
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    fn take(&mut self) -> String {
        let raw = std::mem::take(&mut self.buf);
        raw.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ")
    }

    fn push_nonempty(&mut self, text: String, make: impl FnOnce(String) -> Block) {
        if !text.is_empty() {
            self.blocks.push(make(text));
        }
    }

    fn flush_item_text(&mut self) {
        if self.item_depth > 0 {
            let text = self.take();
            self.push_nonempty(text, Block::ListItem);
        }
    }

    fn finish(mut self) -> ParsedDocument {
        let rest = self.take();
        self.push_nonempty(rest, Block::Paragraph);
        let title = self.blocks.iter().find_map(|block| match block {
            Block::Heading { level: 1, text } => Some(text.clone()),
            _ => None,
        });
        ParsedDocument { title, blocks: self.blocks }
    }
}

fn heading_level(level: HeadingLevel) -> u8 {
    match level {
        HeadingLevel::H1 => 1,
        HeadingLevel::H2 => 2,
        HeadingLevel::H3 => 3,
        HeadingLevel::H4 => 4,
        HeadingLevel::H5 => 5,
        HeadingLevel::H6 => 6,
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::domain::document::{Block, ParsedDocument};

    fn words(text: &str) -> usize {
        text.split_whitespace().count()
    }

    fn doc(blocks: Vec<Block>) -> ParsedDocument {
        ParsedDocument { title: None, blocks }
    }

    fn h(level: u8, text: &str) -> Block {
        Block::Heading { level, text: text.into() }
    }

    fn p(text: &str) -> Block {
        Block::Paragraph(text.into())
    }

    fn chunk(d: &ParsedDocument, cfg: &ChunkerConfig, count: &dyn Fn(&str) -> usize) -> Vec<ChunkDraft> {
        chunk_document(d, cfg, count).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn headings_start_new_chunks_and_form_breadcrumbs() {
        let d = doc(vec![h(1, "Guide"), p("a b c"), h(2, "Install"), p("d e")]);
        let chunks = chunk(&d, &ChunkerConfig::default(), &words);
        assert_eq!(chunks.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(chunks[0].heading_path, vec!["Guide"]);
        assert_eq!(chunks[0].text, "a b c");
        assert_eq!(chunks[1].heading_path, vec!["Guide", "Install"]);
        assert_eq!(chunks[1].embedding_text(), "Guide > Install\n\nd e");
    }

    #[test]
    fn sibling_heading_replaces_previous_sibling() {
        let d = doc(vec![h(1, "A"), h(2, "B"), p("x"), h(2, "C"), p("y")]);
        let chunks = chunk(&d, &ChunkerConfig::default(), &words);
        assert_eq!(chunks[1].heading_path, vec!["A", "C"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn small_blocks_merge_up_to_target() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 5, max_tokens: 8, overlap_tokens: 2 };
        let d = doc(vec![p("a b c"), p("d e"), p("f g h")]);
        let texts: Vec<String> = chunk(&d, &cfg, &words).into_iter().map(|c| c.text).collect();
        assert_eq!(texts, vec!["a b c\n\nd e", "f g h"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn oversized_block_splits_with_overlap_within_max() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 4, max_tokens: 5, overlap_tokens: 2 };
        let text: Vec<String> = (1..=12).map(|i| format!("w{i}")).collect();
        let d = doc(vec![p(&text.join(" "))]);
        let texts: Vec<String> = chunk(&d, &cfg, &words).into_iter().map(|c| c.text).collect();
        assert_eq!(
            texts,
            vec!["w1 w2 w3 w4 w5", "w4 w5 w6 w7 w8", "w7 w8 w9 w10 w11", "w10 w11 w12"]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn unit_that_cannot_fit_is_an_explicit_error() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 2, max_tokens: 3, overlap_tokens: 1 };
        let long_word =
            |t: &str| t.split_whitespace().map(|w| if w == "huge" { 10 } else { 1 }).sum::<usize>();
        let d = doc(vec![p("a huge b")]);
        assert!(matches!(chunk_document(&d, &cfg, &long_word), Err(crate::error::OragError::InvalidInput(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn budget_includes_the_heading_breadcrumb() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 6, max_tokens: 6, overlap_tokens: 0 };
        let d = doc(vec![h(1, "Long Section Title"), p("a b c d e f")]);
        for chunk in chunk(&d, &cfg, &words) {
            assert!(words(&chunk.embedding_text()) <= 6, "{chunk:?}");
            assert_eq!(chunk.token_count, words(&chunk.embedding_text()));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn very_long_words_are_split_without_altering_the_text() {
        // ~1 token per 10 characters, like a BPE tokenizer on a base64 blob.
        let chars_tokens = |t: &str| t.split_whitespace().map(|w| w.chars().count().div_ceil(10)).sum::<usize>();
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 8, max_tokens: 8, overlap_tokens: 0 };
        let blob = "x".repeat(150);
        let chunks = chunk(&doc(vec![p(&blob)]), &cfg, &chars_tokens);
        let lengths: Vec<usize> = chunks.iter().map(|c| c.text.len()).collect();
        assert_eq!(lengths, vec![80, 70]);
        assert_eq!(chunks.iter().map(|c| c.text.as_str()).collect::<String>(), blob, "no invented spaces");
        assert!(chunks.iter().all(|c| c.token_count <= 8));
    }

    #[test]
    fn split_windows_are_exact_slices_of_the_source() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 3, max_tokens: 3, overlap_tokens: 0 };
        let text = "alpha  beta\tgamma delta";
        let chunks = chunk(&doc(vec![p(text)]), &cfg, &words);
        assert_eq!(chunks[0].text, "alpha  beta\tgamma");
        assert_eq!(chunks[1].text, "delta");
    }

    #[test]
    fn oversized_breadcrumb_is_trimmed_by_tokens() {
        let cfg = ChunkerConfig { target_tokens: 8, max_tokens: 8, overlap_tokens: 0 };
        let d = doc(vec![h(1, "one two three four five six"), p("body")]);
        let chunks = chunk(&d, &cfg, &words);
        assert_eq!(chunks[0].breadcrumb, "one two");
        assert_eq!(chunks[0].heading_path, vec!["one two three four five six"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ordinals_are_sequential_and_empty_doc_yields_nothing() {
        assert!(chunk(&doc(vec![]), &ChunkerConfig::default(), &words).is_empty());
        let d = doc(vec![h(1, "A"), p("x"), h(1, "B"), p("y"), h(1, "C"), p("z")]);
        let ordinals: Vec<u32> = chunk(&d, &ChunkerConfig::default(), &words).iter().map(|c| c.ordinal).collect();
        assert_eq!(ordinals, vec![0, 1, 2]);
    }
}
```

`unit_that_cannot_fit_is_an_explicit_error`: `huge` costs 10 tokens against a 3-token budget. A chunk can never exceed the budget, so the chunker returns an error instead of silently overflowing. In production `effective_chunker` (Task 14) guarantees the budget is far above one 16-character unit.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chunker.rs`**

```rust
//! Structure-aware chunking: headings bound chunks, small blocks merge up to a
//! token target, oversized blocks split into overlapping windows.

use crate::domain::document::{Block, ParsedDocument};
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};

/// Bump when chunk boundaries change; part of the embedding-space fingerprint.
pub const CHUNKER_VERSION: u32 = 1;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ChunkerConfig {
    pub target_tokens: usize,
    pub max_tokens: usize,
    pub overlap_tokens: usize,
}

impl Default for ChunkerConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self { target_tokens: 384, max_tokens: 512, overlap_tokens: 48 }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ChunkDraft {
    pub ordinal: u32,
    /// Full heading path, used for citations.
    pub heading_path: Vec<String>,
    /// Heading path as embedded: `A > B`, token-trimmed to a quarter of the budget.
    pub breadcrumb: String,
    pub text: String,
    pub token_count: usize,
}

/// Share of the chunk budget a heading breadcrumb may use.
const BREADCRUMB_BUDGET_DIVISOR: usize = 4;
/// Whitespace-free runs (base64, URLs, hashes) are cut into units of at most
/// this many characters, so every unit costs at most this many tokens.
const MAX_UNIT_CHARS: usize = 16;

/// Exactly the text the embedder receives for a chunk.
pub fn compose_embedding_text(breadcrumb: &str, body: &str) -> String {
    if breadcrumb.is_empty() { body.to_string() } else { format!("{breadcrumb}\n\n{body}") }
}

impl ChunkDraft {
    /// Text given to the embedder; `token_count` measures exactly this string.
    /// The stored/cited text is `self.text`, an unmodified slice of the source.
    pub fn embedding_text(&self) -> String {
        compose_embedding_text(&self.breadcrumb, &self.text)
    }
}

/// `count_tokens` must count tokens of the full string it is given. Every
/// budget check runs on the composed embedding text, so no chunk exceeds
/// `max_tokens`. Errors only when the budget cannot hold one 16-character unit.
pub fn chunk_document(
    doc: &ParsedDocument,
    cfg: &ChunkerConfig,
    count_tokens: &dyn Fn(&str) -> usize,
) -> Result<Vec<ChunkDraft>> {
    let mut builder = ChunkBuilder {
        cfg: *cfg,
        count_tokens,
        headings: Vec::new(),
        breadcrumb: String::new(),
        current: Vec::new(),
        chunks: Vec::new(),
    };
    for block in &doc.blocks {
        match block {
            Block::Heading { level, text } => builder.enter_heading(*level, text),
            other => builder.add_text(other.text())?,
        }
    }
    builder.flush();
    Ok(builder.chunks)
}

struct ChunkBuilder<'a> {
    cfg: ChunkerConfig,
    count_tokens: &'a dyn Fn(&str) -> usize,
    headings: Vec<(u8, String)>,
    breadcrumb: String,
    current: Vec<String>,
    chunks: Vec<ChunkDraft>,
}

impl ChunkBuilder<'_> {
    fn heading_path(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.headings.iter().map(|(_, text)| text.clone()).collect()
    }

    /// Tokens of `body` as the embedder will see it under the current breadcrumb.
    fn cost(&self, body: &str) -> usize {
        (self.count_tokens)(&compose_embedding_text(&self.breadcrumb, body))
    }

    fn enter_heading(&mut self, level: u8, text: &str) {
        self.flush();
        self.headings.retain(|(existing, _)| *existing < level);
        self.headings.push((level, text.to_string()));
        self.breadcrumb = self.trimmed_breadcrumb();
    }

    /// Full `A > B > C` path, cut (by tokens) to a quarter of the chunk budget.
    fn trimmed_breadcrumb(&self) -> String {
        let full = self.heading_path().join(" > ");
        let budget = (self.cfg.max_tokens / BREADCRUMB_BUDGET_DIVISOR).max(1);
        if (self.count_tokens)(&full) <= budget {
            return full;
        }
        let chars: Vec<char> = full.chars().collect();
        let prefix = |n: usize| chars[..n].iter().collect::<String>().trim_end().to_string();
        let (mut lo, mut hi) = (0, chars.len());
        while lo < hi {
            let mid = (lo + hi).div_ceil(2);
            if (self.count_tokens)(&prefix(mid)) <= budget { lo = mid } else { hi = mid - 1 }
        }
        prefix(lo)
    }

    fn add_text(&mut self, text: &str) -> Result<()> {
        if self.cost(text) > self.cfg.max_tokens {
            self.flush();
            for piece in self.split_oversized(text)? {
                self.current.push(piece);
                self.flush();
            }
            return Ok(());
        }
        if !self.current.is_empty() {
            let candidate = format!("{}\n\n{}", self.current.join("\n\n"), text);
            if self.cost(&candidate) > self.cfg.target_tokens {
                self.flush();
            }
        }
        self.current.push(text.to_string());
        Ok(())
    }

    fn flush(&mut self) {
        if self.current.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let text = std::mem::take(&mut self.current).join("\n\n");
        let token_count = self.cost(&text);
        // More than u32::MAX chunks per document is impossible under the upload limit.
        let ordinal = u32::try_from(self.chunks.len()).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
        let (heading_path, breadcrumb) = (self.heading_path(), self.breadcrumb.clone());
        self.chunks.push(ChunkDraft { ordinal, heading_path, breadcrumb, text, token_count });
    }

    /// Cuts `text` into windows that are exact slices of the original (no
    /// invented spaces) and whose composed text fits `max_tokens`. Consecutive
    /// windows overlap by roughly `overlap_tokens`.
    fn split_oversized(&self, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let units = unit_spans(text);
        let slice = |from: usize, to: usize| &text[units[from].0..units[to - 1].1];
        let fits = |from: usize, to: usize| self.cost(slice(from, to)) <= self.cfg.max_tokens;
        let mut pieces = Vec::new();
        let mut start = 0;
        while start < units.len() {
            if !fits(start, start + 1) {
                return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
                    "a {}-token chunk budget cannot hold one {MAX_UNIT_CHARS}-character text unit; \
                     the embedding model's max_tokens is too small",
                    self.cfg.max_tokens
                )));
            }
            let end = largest_fitting_end(start, units.len(), &fits);
            pieces.push(slice(start, end).to_string());
            if end == units.len() {
                break;
            }
            let mut back = end;
            let mut overlap = 0;
            while back > start + 1 {
                let cost = (self.count_tokens)(slice(back - 1, back)).max(1);
                if overlap + cost > self.cfg.overlap_tokens {
                    break;
                }
                overlap += cost;
                back -= 1;
            }
            start = back;
        }
        Ok(pieces)
    }
}

/// Largest `end` in `(start, len]` with `fits(start, end)`, given that
/// `fits(start, start + 1)` holds. Exponential probing keeps each search near
/// the window size, so total work is ~ text length × log(window), not quadratic.
fn largest_fitting_end(start: usize, len: usize, fits: &dyn Fn(usize, usize) -> bool) -> usize {
    let mut good = start + 1;
    let mut step = 1;
    loop {
        let probe = (good + step).min(len);
        if probe == good {
            return good;
        }
        if fits(start, probe) {
            good = probe;
            step *= 2;
            continue;
        }
        let (mut lo, mut hi) = (good, probe - 1);
        while lo < hi {
            let mid = (lo + hi).div_ceil(2);
            if fits(start, mid) { lo = mid } else { hi = mid - 1 }
        }
        return lo;
    }
}

/// Byte spans of whitespace-separated words; words longer than
/// `MAX_UNIT_CHARS` are cut at character boundaries into adjacent spans.
fn unit_spans(text: &str) -> Vec<(usize, usize)> {
    let mut spans = Vec::new();
    let mut word_start: Option<usize> = None;
    for (index, ch) in text.char_indices().chain(std::iter::once((text.len(), ' '))) {
        if ch.is_whitespace() {
            if let Some(start) = word_start.take() {
                push_word_units(text, start, index, &mut spans);
            }
        } else if word_start.is_none() {
            word_start = Some(index);
        }
    }
    spans
}

fn push_word_units(text: &str, start: usize, end: usize, spans: &mut Vec<(usize, usize)>) {
    let mut unit_start = start;
    for (count, (offset, _)) in text[start..end].char_indices().enumerate() {
        if count > 0 && count % MAX_UNIT_CHARS == 0 {
            spans.push((unit_start, start + offset));
            unit_start = start + offset;
        }
    }
    spans.push((unit_start, end));
}
```

        let one = &hits[0];

    }

`domain/rrf.rs`:

```rust
//! Reciprocal Rank Fusion: score(d) = Σ 1 / (k + rank_i(d)), rank 1-based.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use serde::Serialize;

use crate::domain::ChunkId;

pub const RRF_K: f64 = 60.0;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct FusedHit {
    pub chunk_id: ChunkId,
    pub score: f64,
    pub lexical_rank: Option<usize>,
    pub dense_rank: Option<usize>,
}

/// Fuses two ranked lists. Sorted by score descending, ties by chunk id ascending.
pub fn reciprocal_rank_fusion(lexical: &[ChunkId], dense: &[ChunkId], k: f64) -> Vec<FusedHit> {
    let mut hits: Vec<FusedHit> = Vec::new();
    let mut slots: HashMap<ChunkId, usize> = HashMap::new();
    for (list, is_lexical) in [(lexical, true), (dense, false)] {
        for (position, &chunk_id) in list.iter().enumerate() {
            let slot = *slots.entry(chunk_id).or_insert_with(|| {
                hits.push(FusedHit { chunk_id, score: 0.0, lexical_rank: None, dense_rank: None });
                hits.len() - 1
            });
            let hit = &mut hits[slot];
            let rank_slot = if is_lexical { &mut hit.lexical_rank } else { &mut hit.dense_rank };
            if rank_slot.is_some() {
                continue; // duplicate within one list: keep the best (first) rank
            }
            let rank = position + 1;
            *rank_slot = Some(rank);
            hit.score += 1.0 / (k + rank as f64);
        }
    }
    hits.sort_by(|a, b| b.score.total_cmp(&a.score).then(a.chunk_id.cmp(&b.chunk_id)));
    hits
}
```

`domain/citations.rs`:

```rust
//! Extraction and validation of `[n]` citation markers in generated answers.

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Citations {
    /// 1-based source numbers that exist, unique, in first-seen order.
    pub valid: Vec<usize>,
    /// Numbers cited by the model that do not correspond to a source.
    pub invalid: Vec<usize>,
}

/// Finds `[n]` markers (inner whitespace allowed) and checks `1 <= n <= source_count`.
pub fn extract_citations(answer: &str, source_count: usize) -> Citations {
    let mut citations = Citations::default();
    let mut rest = answer;
    while let Some(open) = rest.find('[') {
        let after = &rest[open + 1..];
        let Some(close) = after.find(']') else { break };
        let inner = after[..close].trim();
        if !inner.is_empty() && inner.len() <= 6 && inner.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
            if let Ok(number) = inner.parse::<usize>() {
                let bucket = if (1..=source_count).contains(&number) {
                    &mut citations.valid
                } else {
                    &mut citations.invalid
                };
                if !bucket.contains(&number) {
                    bucket.push(number);
                }
            }
        }
        rest = &after[close + 1..];
    }
    citations
}
```

```sql
-- Schema version 1. Never edit after release; add 0002_*.sql instead.
-- AUTOINCREMENT on mutable entities: ids are never reused after deletion, so a
-- stale job or citation can never address a newer row.

CREATE TABLE embedding_spaces (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
    fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    model_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    dimensions INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (dimensions > 0),
    descriptor TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now'))
);

CREATE TABLE collections (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    embedding_space_id INTEGER REFERENCES embedding_spaces(id),
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now'))
);

-- Immutable source snapshots, shared across collections by content hash.
CREATE TABLE sources (
    sha256 TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    size_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL,
    bytes BLOB NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE documents (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    collection_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES collections(id),
    source_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources(sha256),
    filename TEXT,
    format TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT,
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('queued', 'indexing', 'ready', 'failed')),
    error TEXT,
    chunk_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    warnings TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')),
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')),
    UNIQUE (collection_id, source_sha256)
);
CREATE INDEX documents_collection ON documents(collection_id, id);

CREATE TABLE jobs (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    document_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('ingest')),
    status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ('queued', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed')),
    error TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')),
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now'))
);
CREATE INDEX jobs_queue ON jobs(status, id);

-- No ON DELETE CASCADE: chunk deletion must go through store code so FTS and
-- vector rows are removed in the same transaction (the FK makes forgetting fail).
CREATE TABLE chunks (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    document_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES documents(id),
    collection_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES collections(id),
    ordinal INTEGER NOT NULL,
    heading_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    text TEXT NOT NULL,
    token_count INTEGER NOT NULL,
    UNIQUE (document_id, ordinal)
);
CREATE INDEX chunks_collection ON chunks(collection_id);

-- Normalized shadow text only (D-006); rowid == chunks.id.
CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE chunks_fts USING fts5(
    norm_text,
    content = '',
    contentless_delete = 1,
    tokenize = 'unicode61 remove_diacritics 0'
);

INSERT INTO collections (name) VALUES ('default');
```

        drop(store);
        let err = Store::open(&dir.path().join("orag.db")).err().unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(err, OragError::SchemaTooNew { found: 99, .. }), "{err}");

        migrations::migrate_with(&mut conn, &path, &["CREATE TABLE a (x);"]).unwrap();
        let version =

        assert!(dir.path().join("t.db.pre-schema-2.bak").exists());

        let name: String = copy.query_row("SELECT name FROM collections", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(name, "default");
        assert!(store.backup_to(&dest).is_err(), "must not overwrite an existing file");
    }
}

`store/migrations.rs`:

```rust
//! Ordered, transactional, embedded schema migrations (D-017).

use std::path::Path;

use rusqlite::Connection;

use crate::error::{OragError, Result};

pub const MIGRATIONS: &[&str] = &[include_str!("migrations/0001_initial.sql")];
pub const SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = MIGRATIONS.len() as u32;

pub fn migrate(conn: &mut Connection, db_path: &Path) -> Result<u32> {
    migrate_with(conn, db_path, MIGRATIONS)
}

/// Applies `migrations[user_version..]`, one transaction each. Refuses a newer
/// schema; backs up an existing database before changing it.
pub fn migrate_with(conn: &mut Connection, db_path: &Path, migrations: &[&str]) -> Result<u32> {
    let current: u32 = conn.pragma_query_value(None, "user_version", |row| row.get(0))?;
    let target = migrations.len() as u32;
    if current > target {
        return Err(OragError::SchemaTooNew { found: current, supported: target });
    }
    if current == target {
        return Ok(current);
    }
    if current > 0 {
        let file_name = db_path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()).unwrap_or("orag.db");
        let backup = db_path.with_file_name(format!("{file_name}.pre-schema-{target}.bak"));
        conn.execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [backup.to_string_lossy()])?;
    }
    for (index, sql) in migrations.iter().enumerate().skip(current as usize) {
        let tx = conn.transaction()?;
        tx.execute_batch(sql)?;
        tx.pragma_update(None, "user_version", index as u32 + 1)?;
        tx.commit()?;
    }
    Ok(target)
}
```

`store/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! SQLite storage (D-003). One writer connection behind a mutex; readers open
//! short-lived read-only connections (WAL allows concurrent readers).
//! All methods block: async callers must use `tokio::task::spawn_blocking`.

pub mod migrations;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Mutex, Once};
use std::time::Duration;

use rusqlite::{Connection, OpenFlags};

use crate::error::{OragError, Result};

pub struct Store {
    path: PathBuf,
    writer: Mutex<Connection>,
    schema_version: u32,
}

impl Store {
    pub fn open(path: &Path) -> Result<Store> {
        register_sqlite_vec();
        let mut writer = open_connection(path, false)?;
        let schema_version = migrations::migrate(&mut writer, path)?;
        Ok(Store { path: path.to_path_buf(), writer: Mutex::new(writer), schema_version })
    }

    pub fn path(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.path
    }

    pub fn schema_version(&self) -> u32 {
        self.schema_version
    }

    pub(crate) fn write<T>(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut Connection) -> Result<T>) -> Result<T> {
        let mut conn = self
            .writer
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| OragError::Internal("database writer lock poisoned".into()))?;
        f(&mut conn)
    }

    /// Consistent online backup via `VACUUM INTO` (D-004). Refuses to overwrite `dest`.
    pub fn backup_to(&self, dest: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        if dest.exists() {
            return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!("{} already exists", dest.display())));
        }
        self.write(|conn| {
            conn.execute("VACUUM INTO ?1", [dest.to_string_lossy()])?;
            Ok(())
        })
    }
}

fn open_connection(path: &Path, read_only: bool) -> Result<Connection> {
    let base = OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_NO_MUTEX | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_URI;
    let flags = if read_only {
        base | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_ONLY
    } else {
        base | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_READ_WRITE | OpenFlags::SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE
    };
    let conn = Connection::open_with_flags(path, flags)?;
    conn.busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))?;
    conn.pragma_update(None, "foreign_keys", true)?;
    if !read_only {
        let mode: String =
            conn.pragma_update_and_check(None, "journal_mode", "WAL", |row| row.get(0))?;
        if !mode.eq_ignore_ascii_case("wal") {
            return Err(OragError::Internal(format!("could not enable WAL (got {mode})")));
        }
        conn.pragma_update(None, "synchronous", "NORMAL")?;
    }
    Ok(conn)
}

type ExtensionInit = unsafe extern "C" fn(
    *mut rusqlite::ffi::sqlite3,
    *mut *mut std::os::raw::c_char,
    *const rusqlite::ffi::sqlite3_api_routines,
) -> std::os::raw::c_int;

/// Registers sqlite-vec for every connection opened afterwards in this process.
fn register_sqlite_vec() {
    static REGISTER: Once = Once::new();
    REGISTER.call_once(|| {
        // SAFETY: `sqlite3_vec_init` is the extension entry point compiled into
        // this binary by the sqlite-vec crate; its signature matches `ExtensionInit`.
        unsafe {
            let init = std::mem::transmute::<*const (), ExtensionInit>(
                sqlite_vec::sqlite3_vec_init as *const (),
            );
            rusqlite::ffi::sqlite3_auto_extension(Some(init));
        }
    });
}
```

/// ISO-8601 UTC timestamp expression used in UPDATE statements.
pub(crate) const NOW_SQL: &str = "strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', 'now')";

pub(crate) fn is_unique_violation(err: &rusqlite::Error) -> bool {
    matches!(err, rusqlite::Error::SqliteFailure(e, _) if e.code == rusqlite::ErrorCode::ConstraintViolation)
}

    /// Short-lived read-only connection; WAL lets it run beside the writer.
    pub(crate) fn read(&self) -> Result<Connection> {
        open_connection(&self.path, true)

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::store::testing::temp_store;

    fn md(name: &str, body: &str) -> NewDocument {
        NewDocument { filename: Some(name.into()), format: SourceFormat::Markdown, bytes: body.as_bytes().to_vec() }
    }

    #[test]
    fn enqueue_creates_queued_document_and_job() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let e = store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", "# A")).unwrap();
        assert!(!e.duplicate);
        let doc = store.get_document(1, e.document_id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(doc.status, DocumentStatus::Queued);
        assert_eq!(doc.filename.as_deref(), Some("a.md"));
        assert_eq!(doc.size_bytes, 3);
        assert_eq!(store.get_job(e.job_id).unwrap().status, crate::store::jobs::JobStatus::Queued);
    }

    #[test]
    fn same_content_in_same_collection_is_a_duplicate() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let first = store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", "same")).unwrap();
        let second = store.enqueue_document(1, md("renamed.md", "same")).unwrap();
        assert!(second.duplicate);
        assert_eq!((second.document_id, second.job_id), (first.document_id, first.job_id));
    }

    #[test]
    fn same_content_in_other_collection_shares_the_source() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let other = store.create_collection("other").unwrap();
        store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", "same")).unwrap();
        let e = store.enqueue_document(other.id, md("a.md", "same")).unwrap();
        assert!(!e.duplicate);
        let sources: i64 = store.read().unwrap().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sources", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(sources, 1);
    }

    #[test]

        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let e = store.enqueue_document(1, md("../../etc/notes.md", "x")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(store.get_document(1, e.document_id).unwrap().filename.as_deref(), Some("notes.md"));
        let e = store.enqueue_document(1, md("C:\\Users\\a\\b.md", "y")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(store.get_document(1, e.document_id).unwrap().filename.as_deref(), Some("b.md"));
        assert!(store.enqueue_document(1, md("bad\u{7}.md", "z")).is_err());
    }

        assert!(matches!(store.enqueue_document(9, md("a.md", "x")), Err(OragError::NotFound { kind: "collection", .. })));

        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let other = store.create_collection("other").unwrap();
        let e = store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", "x")).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(store.get_document(other.id, e.document_id), Err(OragError::NotFound { kind: "document", .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn list_paginates_by_id() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let ids: Vec<i64> = (0..5)
            .map(|i| store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", &format!("doc {i}"))).unwrap().document_id)
            .collect();
        let first = store.list_documents(1, Page { after_id: None, limit: 2 }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(first.iter().map(|d| d.id).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ids[..2]);
        let next = store.list_documents(1, Page { after_id: Some(ids[1]), limit: 10 }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(next.iter().map(|d| d.id).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ids[2..]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn load_source_returns_original_bytes() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let e = store.enqueue_document(1, md("a.md", "# Başlık")).unwrap();
        let (doc, bytes) = store.load_source(e.document_id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(doc.id, e.document_id);
        assert_eq!(bytes, "# Başlık".as_bytes());
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::ingest::format::SourceFormat;
    use crate::store::documents::{DocumentStatus, NewDocument};
    use crate::store::testing::temp_store;

    fn enqueue(store: &crate::store::Store, body: &str) -> crate::store::documents::Enqueued {
        store
            .enqueue_document(1, NewDocument { filename: None, format: SourceFormat::PlainText, bytes: body.into() })
            .unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn claim_takes_oldest_and_marks_running_and_indexing() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let first = enqueue(&store, "one");
        enqueue(&store, "two");
        let job = store.claim_next_job().unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(job.id, first.job_id);
        assert_eq!(job.status, JobStatus::Running);
        assert_eq!(job.collection_id, 1);
        assert_eq!(store.get_document(1, first.document_id).unwrap().status, DocumentStatus::Indexing);
    }

    #[test]
    fn claim_returns_none_when_queue_is_empty() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        assert!(store.claim_next_job().unwrap().is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fail_marks_job_and_document() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let e = enqueue(&store, "x");
        let job = store.claim_next_job().unwrap().unwrap();
        store.fail_job(&job, "parser exploded").unwrap();
        let job = store.get_job(e.job_id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((job.status, job.error.as_deref()), (JobStatus::Failed, Some("parser exploded")));
        let doc = store.get_document(1, e.document_id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((doc.status, doc.error.as_deref()), (DocumentStatus::Failed, Some("parser exploded")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn requeue_moves_running_jobs_back_to_queue() {
        let (_dir, store) = temp_store();
        let e = enqueue(&store, "x");
        store.claim_next_job().unwrap().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(store.requeue_running_jobs().unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(store.get_job(e.job_id).unwrap().status, JobStatus::Queued);
        assert_eq!(store.get_document(1, e.document_id).unwrap().status, DocumentStatus::Queued);
    }
}

```rust
//! Collections: named, isolated document sets (D-014).

use rusqlite::{OptionalExtension, Row};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::domain::CollectionId;
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};
use crate::store::{Store, is_unique_violation};

pub const DEFAULT_COLLECTION: &str = "default";
const MAX_NAME_CHARS: usize = 64;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Collection {
    pub id: CollectionId,
    pub name: String,
    pub document_count: i64,
    pub created_at: String,
}

const SELECT_COLLECTION: &str = "SELECT c.id, c.name, \
     (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM documents d WHERE d.collection_id = c.id), c.created_at \
     FROM collections c";

fn collection_from_row(row: &Row<'_>) -> rusqlite::Result<Collection> {
    Ok(Collection { id: row.get(0)?, name: row.get(1)?, document_count: row.get(2)?, created_at: row.get(3)? })
}

/// 1–64 characters: letters, digits, space, `_`, `.`, `-`; no leading/trailing space.
pub fn validate_collection_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let length = name.chars().count();
    let allowed = |c: char| c.is_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, ' ' | '_' | '.' | '-');
    if length == 0 || length > MAX_NAME_CHARS || name.trim() != name || !name.chars().all(allowed) {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput(format!(
            "collection name must be 1-{MAX_NAME_CHARS} letters, digits, spaces, '_', '.' or '-' without surrounding spaces"
        )));
    }
    Ok(())
}

impl Store {
    pub fn create_collection(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Collection> {
        validate_collection_name(name)?;
        self.write(|conn| {
            match conn.execute("INSERT INTO collections (name) VALUES (?1)", [name]) {
                Ok(_) => {}
                Err(err) if is_unique_violation(&err) => {
                    return Err(OragError::Conflict(format!("collection `{name}` already exists")));
                }
                Err(err) => return Err(err.into()),
            }
            let id = conn.last_insert_rowid();
            Ok(conn.query_row(&format!("{SELECT_COLLECTION} WHERE c.id = ?1"), [id], collection_from_row)?)
        })
    }

    pub fn list_collections(&self) -> Result<Vec<Collection>> {
        let conn = self.read()?;
        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(&format!("{SELECT_COLLECTION} ORDER BY c.id"))?;
        let rows = stmt.query_map([], collection_from_row)?;
        Ok(rows.collect::<rusqlite::Result<Vec<_>>>()?)
    }

    pub fn get_collection(&self, id: CollectionId) -> Result<Collection> {
        let conn = self.read()?;
        conn.query_row(&format!("{SELECT_COLLECTION} WHERE c.id = ?1"), [id], collection_from_row)
            .optional()?
            .ok_or(OragError::NotFound { kind: "collection", id })
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Documents and their immutable source snapshots.

use rusqlite::types::Type;
use rusqlite::{OptionalExtension, Row, Transaction, params};
use serde::Serialize;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use crate::domain::{CollectionId, DocumentId, JobId};
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};
use crate::ingest::format::SourceFormat;
use crate::store::Store;

const MAX_FILENAME_CHARS: usize = 255;
pub const MAX_PAGE_SIZE: u32 = 200;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum DocumentStatus {
    Queued,
    Indexing,
    Ready,
    Failed,
}

impl DocumentStatus {
    fn parse(text: &str) -> Option<DocumentStatus> {
        match text {
            "queued" => Some(DocumentStatus::Queued),
            "indexing" => Some(DocumentStatus::Indexing),
            "ready" => Some(DocumentStatus::Ready),
            "failed" => Some(DocumentStatus::Failed),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct DocumentRecord {
    pub id: DocumentId,
    pub collection_id: CollectionId,
    pub filename: Option<String>,
    pub format: String,
    pub title: Option<String>,
    pub status: DocumentStatus,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub chunk_count: i64,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
    pub source_sha256: String,
    pub size_bytes: i64,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub updated_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct NewDocument {
    pub filename: Option<String>,
    pub format: SourceFormat,
    pub bytes: Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct Enqueued {
    pub document_id: DocumentId,
    pub job_id: JobId,
    pub duplicate: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Page {
    pub after_id: Option<i64>,
    pub limit: u32,
}

pub(crate) const SELECT_DOCUMENT: &str = "SELECT d.id, d.collection_id, d.filename, d.format, d.title, \
     d.status, d.error, d.chunk_count, d.warnings, d.source_sha256, s.size_bytes, d.created_at, \
     d.updated_at FROM documents d JOIN sources s ON s.sha256 = d.source_sha256";

fn conversion_error(index: usize, message: String) -> rusqlite::Error {
    rusqlite::Error::FromSqlConversionFailure(index, Type::Text, message.into())
}

pub(crate) fn document_from_row(row: &Row<'_>) -> rusqlite::Result<DocumentRecord> {
    let status: String = row.get(5)?;
    let warnings: String = row.get(8)?;
    Ok(DocumentRecord {
        id: row.get(0)?,
        collection_id: row.get(1)?,
        filename: row.get(2)?,
        format: row.get(3)?,
        title: row.get(4)?,
        status: DocumentStatus::parse(&status)
            .ok_or_else(|| conversion_error(5, format!("unknown status {status}")))?,
        error: row.get(6)?,
        chunk_count: row.get(7)?,
        warnings: serde_json::from_str(&warnings).map_err(|e| conversion_error(8, e.to_string()))?,
        source_sha256: row.get(9)?,
        size_bytes: row.get(10)?,
        created_at: row.get(11)?,
        updated_at: row.get(12)?,
    })
}

/// Keeps only the last path component and rejects control characters.
fn clean_filename(filename: Option<String>) -> Result<Option<String>> {
    let Some(raw) = filename else { return Ok(None) };
    let base = raw.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string();
    if base.is_empty() || base == "." || base == ".." {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if base.chars().count() > MAX_FILENAME_CHARS || base.chars().any(char::is_control) {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput("filename is too long or contains control characters".into()));
    }
    Ok(Some(base))
}

pub(crate) fn ensure_collection(tx: &Transaction<'_>, collection_id: CollectionId) -> Result<()> {
    tx.query_row("SELECT 1 FROM collections WHERE id = ?1", [collection_id], |_| Ok(()))
        .optional()?
        .ok_or(OragError::NotFound { kind: "collection", id: collection_id })
}

impl Store {
    /// Stores the source snapshot and queues an ingest job. Identical content in
    /// the same collection returns the existing document (`duplicate: true`).
    pub fn enqueue_document(&self, collection_id: CollectionId, doc: NewDocument) -> Result<Enqueued> {
        if doc.bytes.is_empty() {
            return Err(OragError::InvalidInput("document is empty".into()));
        }
        let filename = clean_filename(doc.filename)?;
        let sha = hex::encode(Sha256::digest(&doc.bytes));
        self.write(|conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction()?;
            ensure_collection(&tx, collection_id)?;
            let existing = tx
                .query_row(
                    "SELECT d.id, (SELECT MAX(j.id) FROM jobs j WHERE j.document_id = d.id) \
                     FROM documents d WHERE d.collection_id = ?1 AND d.source_sha256 = ?2",
                    params![collection_id, sha],
                    |row| Ok((row.get::<_, i64>(0)?, row.get::<_, i64>(1)?)),
                )
                .optional()?;
            if let Some((document_id, job_id)) = existing {
                tx.commit()?;
                return Ok(Enqueued { document_id, job_id, duplicate: true });
            }
            tx.execute(
                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sources (sha256, size_bytes, bytes) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
                params![sha, doc.bytes.len() as i64, doc.bytes],
            )?;
            tx.execute(
                "INSERT INTO documents (collection_id, source_sha256, filename, format, status) \
                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, 'queued')",
                params![collection_id, sha, filename, doc.format.as_str()],
            )?;
            let document_id = tx.last_insert_rowid();
            tx.execute("INSERT INTO jobs (document_id, kind, status) VALUES (?1, 'ingest', 'queued')", [document_id])?;
            let job_id = tx.last_insert_rowid();
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(Enqueued { document_id, job_id, duplicate: false })
        })
    }

    pub fn get_document(&self, collection_id: CollectionId, document_id: DocumentId) -> Result<DocumentRecord> {
        let conn = self.read()?;
        conn.query_row(
            &format!("{SELECT_DOCUMENT} WHERE d.id = ?1 AND d.collection_id = ?2"),
            params![document_id, collection_id],
            document_from_row,
        )
        .optional()?
        .ok_or(OragError::NotFound { kind: "document", id: document_id })
    }

    pub fn list_documents(&self, collection_id: CollectionId, page: Page) -> Result<Vec<DocumentRecord>> {
        let limit = page.limit.clamp(1, MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        let conn = self.read()?;
        let mut stmt = conn.prepare(&format!(
            "{SELECT_DOCUMENT} WHERE d.collection_id = ?1 AND d.id > ?2 ORDER BY d.id LIMIT ?3"
        ))?;
        let rows = stmt.query_map(params![collection_id, page.after_id.unwrap_or(0), limit], document_from_row)?;
        Ok(rows.collect::<rusqlite::Result<Vec<_>>>()?)
    }

    pub fn load_source(&self, document_id: DocumentId) -> Result<(DocumentRecord, Vec<u8>)> {
        let conn = self.read()?;
        let doc = conn
            .query_row(&format!("{SELECT_DOCUMENT} WHERE d.id = ?1"), [document_id], document_from_row)
            .optional()?
            .ok_or(OragError::NotFound { kind: "document", id: document_id })?;
        let bytes: Vec<u8> =
            conn.query_row("SELECT bytes FROM sources WHERE sha256 = ?1", [&doc.source_sha256], |r| r.get(0))?;
        Ok((doc, bytes))
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Durable ingestion job queue (D-008).

use rusqlite::types::Type;
use rusqlite::{OptionalExtension, Row, params};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::domain::{CollectionId, DocumentId, JobId};
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};
use crate::store::{NOW_SQL, Store};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum JobStatus {
    Queued,
    Running,
    Succeeded,
    Failed,
}

impl JobStatus {
    fn parse(text: &str) -> Option<JobStatus> {
        match text {
            "queued" => Some(JobStatus::Queued),
            "running" => Some(JobStatus::Running),
            "succeeded" => Some(JobStatus::Succeeded),
            "failed" => Some(JobStatus::Failed),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct JobRecord {
    pub id: JobId,
    pub document_id: DocumentId,
    pub collection_id: CollectionId,
    pub status: JobStatus,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub created_at: String,
    pub updated_at: String,
}

const SELECT_JOB: &str = "SELECT j.id, j.document_id, d.collection_id, j.status, j.error, \
     j.created_at, j.updated_at FROM jobs j JOIN documents d ON d.id = j.document_id";

fn job_from_row(row: &Row<'_>) -> rusqlite::Result<JobRecord> {
    let status: String = row.get(3)?;
    Ok(JobRecord {
        id: row.get(0)?,
        document_id: row.get(1)?,
        collection_id: row.get(2)?,
        status: JobStatus::parse(&status).ok_or_else(|| {
            rusqlite::Error::FromSqlConversionFailure(3, Type::Text, format!("unknown status {status}").into())
        })?,
        error: row.get(4)?,
        created_at: row.get(5)?,
        updated_at: row.get(6)?,
    })
}

impl Store {
    pub fn get_job(&self, job_id: JobId) -> Result<JobRecord> {
        let conn = self.read()?;
        conn.query_row(&format!("{SELECT_JOB} WHERE j.id = ?1"), [job_id], job_from_row)
            .optional()?
            .ok_or(OragError::NotFound { kind: "job", id: job_id })
    }

    /// Atomically moves the oldest queued job to `running` and its document to `indexing`.
    pub fn claim_next_job(&self) -> Result<Option<JobRecord>> {
        self.write(|conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction()?;
            let next: Option<(JobId, DocumentId)> = tx
                .query_row(
                    "SELECT id, document_id FROM jobs WHERE status = 'queued' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1",
                    [],
                    |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)),
                )
                .optional()?;
            let Some((job_id, document_id)) = next else {
                tx.commit()?;
                return Ok(None);
            };
            tx.execute(&format!("UPDATE jobs SET status = 'running', updated_at = {NOW_SQL} WHERE id = ?1"), [job_id])?;
            tx.execute(
                &format!("UPDATE documents SET status = 'indexing', error = NULL, updated_at = {NOW_SQL} WHERE id = ?1"),
                [document_id],
            )?;
            let job = tx.query_row(&format!("{SELECT_JOB} WHERE j.id = ?1"), [job_id], job_from_row)?;
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(Some(job))
        })
    }

    /// Records a user-safe failure message on the job and its document. Only a
    /// job still `running` (i.e. still owned by this claim) is changed.
    pub fn fail_job(&self, job: &JobRecord, message: &str) -> Result<()> {
        self.write(|conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction()?;
            let owned = tx.execute(
                &format!(
                    "UPDATE jobs SET status = 'failed', error = ?2, updated_at = {NOW_SQL} \
                     WHERE id = ?1 AND status = 'running'"
                ),
                params![job.id, message],
            )?;
            if owned == 1 {
                tx.execute(
                    &format!(
                        "UPDATE documents SET status = 'failed', error = ?2, updated_at = {NOW_SQL} \
                         WHERE id = ?1 AND status = 'indexing'"
                    ),
                    params![job.document_id, message],
                )?;
            }
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(())
        })
    }

    /// Restart recovery: jobs left `running` by a crash go back to `queued`.
    pub fn requeue_running_jobs(&self) -> Result<usize> {
        self.write(|conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction()?;
            let moved = tx.execute(
                &format!("UPDATE jobs SET status = 'queued', updated_at = {NOW_SQL} WHERE status = 'running'"),
                [],
            )?;
            tx.execute(
                &format!("UPDATE documents SET status = 'queued', updated_at = {NOW_SQL} WHERE status = 'indexing'"),
                [],
            )?;
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(moved)
        })
    }
}
```

            SpaceDescriptor { require_trailing_eos: false, ..descriptor() },

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::infer::Role;
    use std::ops::ControlFlow;

    fn cosine(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
        a.iter().zip(b).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum()
    }

    #[test]
    fn fixture_vectors_win_and_are_normalized() {
        let e = FakeEmbedder::new().with_fixture("iade", vec![3.0, 4.0]);
        let v = e.embed_query("İade süresi?").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v.len(), FAKE_DIMENSIONS);
        assert!((v[0] - 0.6).abs() < 1e-6 && (v[1] - 0.8).abs() < 1e-6);
    }

    #[test]
    fn shared_words_are_more_similar_than_unrelated_text() {
        let e = FakeEmbedder::new();
        let docs = e.embed_documents(&["return policy days".into(), "coffee machine descaling".into()]).unwrap();
        let q = e.embed_query("return policy").unwrap();
        assert!(cosine(&q, &docs[0]) > cosine(&q, &docs[1]));
    }

    #[test]
    fn generator_streams_reply_and_records_request() {
        let g = FakeGenerator::new("Cevap [1] burada.");
        let mut out = String::new();
        let req = GenerationRequest { messages: vec![ChatMessage { role: Role::User, content: "q".into() }], max_output_tokens: 10 };
        let stats = g.generate(&req, &mut |piece| { out.push_str(piece); ControlFlow::Continue(()) }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(out, "Cevap [1] burada.");
        assert_eq!(stats.completion_tokens, 3);
        assert!(!stats.cancelled);
        assert_eq!(g.last_request().unwrap(), req);
    }

    #[test]
    fn generator_stops_when_consumer_breaks() {
        let g = FakeGenerator::new("a b c d e");
        let req = GenerationRequest { messages: vec![], max_output_tokens: 10 };
        let mut seen = 0;
        let stats = g
            .generate(&req, &mut |_| { seen += 1; if seen == 2 { ControlFlow::Break(()) } else { ControlFlow::Continue(()) } })
            .unwrap();
        assert!(stats.cancelled);
        assert_eq!(g.emitted_tokens(), 2);
    }
}
