- [ ] **Step 3: Add `bulk_insert_vectors`** to the `impl Store` in `store/search.rs`

```rust
    /// Benchmark-only: inserts vectors with consecutive rowids starting at `first_rowid`,
    /// without chunk rows. Never call on a user database.
    pub(crate) fn bulk_insert_vectors(
        &self,
        space: &EmbeddingSpace,
        collection_id: CollectionId,
        first_rowid: i64,
        vectors: &[Vec<f32>],
    ) -> Result<()> {
        self.write(|conn| {
            let tx = conn.transaction()?;
            {
                let mut insert = tx.prepare(&format!(
                    "INSERT INTO {} (rowid, collection_id, embedding) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
                    vec_table(space.id)
                ))?;
                for (offset, vector) in vectors.iter().enumerate() {
                    insert.execute(params![first_rowid + offset as i64, collection_id, f32_bytes(vector)])?;
                }
            }
            tx.commit()?;
            Ok(())
        })
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `eval/vector_scale.rs`**

```rust
//! Synthetic dense-search latency benchmark for the v1 scale promise (D-003):
//! 100k chunks × 1024 dims, warm p95 < 250 ms on the reference laptop.

use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Instant;

use serde::Serialize;

use crate::domain::space::SpaceDescriptor;
use crate::error::{OragError, Result};
use crate::eval::metrics::percentile;
use crate::infer::{VectorIndex, l2_normalize};
use crate::store::Store;
use crate::store::search::SqliteVecIndex;

pub const TARGET_P95_MS: f64 = 250.0;
const INSERT_BATCH: usize = 5_000;
const WARMUP_QUERIES: usize = 3;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct VectorScaleConfig {
    pub chunks: usize,
    pub dimensions: usize,
    pub queries: usize,
    pub k: usize,
    pub seed: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct VectorScaleReport {
    /// `<os>-<arch>`, e.g. `macos-aarch64`, `linux-x86_64`.
    pub platform: String,
    pub version: &'static str,
    pub chunks: usize,
    pub dimensions: usize,
    pub queries: usize,
    pub k: usize,
    pub insert_seconds: f64,
    pub p50_ms: f64,
    pub p95_ms: f64,
    pub max_ms: f64,
    pub target_p95_ms: f64,
    pub passed: bool,
}

impl VectorScaleReport {
    pub fn to_markdown(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "| platform | version | chunks | dims | k | queries | insert s | p50 ms | p95 ms | max ms | target p95 | result |\n\
             |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n\
             | {} | {} | {} | {} | {} | {} | {:.1} | {:.1} | {:.1} | {:.1} | {:.0} | {} |\n",
            self.platform, self.version, self.chunks, self.dimensions, self.k, self.queries, self.insert_seconds,
            self.p50_ms, self.p95_ms, self.max_ms, self.target_p95_ms, if self.passed { "PASS" } else { "FAIL" }
        )
    }
}

pub(crate) struct XorShift(pub(crate) u64);

impl XorShift {
    fn next_f32(&mut self) -> f32 {
        self.0 ^= self.0 << 13;
        self.0 ^= self.0 >> 7;
        self.0 ^= self.0 << 17;
        (self.0 >> 40) as f32 / (1u64 << 24) as f32 - 0.5
    }
}

pub(crate) fn random_unit(rng: &mut XorShift, dimensions: usize) -> Vec<f32> {
    let raw: Vec<f32> = (0..dimensions).map(|_| rng.next_f32()).collect();
    l2_normalize(&raw)
}

pub(crate) fn synthetic_descriptor(dimensions: usize) -> SpaceDescriptor {
    SpaceDescriptor {
        model_id: "synthetic-benchmark".into(),
        model_sha256: "0".repeat(64),
        pooling: "none".into(),
        query_prefix: String::new(),
        document_prefix: String::new(),
        dimensions,
        normalized: true,
        max_tokens: 512,
        require_trailing_eos: false,
    }
}

pub fn run_vector_scale(cfg: &VectorScaleConfig, work_dir: &Path) -> Result<VectorScaleReport> {
    if cfg.chunks == 0 || cfg.queries == 0 || cfg.k == 0 {
        return Err(OragError::InvalidInput("chunks, queries and k must be positive".into()));
    }
    let store = Arc::new(Store::open(&work_dir.join("vector-scale.db"))?);
    let space = store.bind_space(1, &synthetic_descriptor(cfg.dimensions))?;
    let mut rng = XorShift(cfg.seed.max(1));
    let started = Instant::now();
    let mut inserted = 0usize;
    while inserted < cfg.chunks {
        let batch = INSERT_BATCH.min(cfg.chunks - inserted);
        let vectors: Vec<Vec<f32>> = (0..batch).map(|_| random_unit(&mut rng, cfg.dimensions)).collect();
        store.bulk_insert_vectors(&space, 1, inserted as i64 + 1, &vectors)?;
        inserted += batch;
    }
    let insert_seconds = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
    let index = SqliteVecIndex::new(store);
    for _ in 0..WARMUP_QUERIES {
        index.search(&space, 1, &random_unit(&mut rng, cfg.dimensions), cfg.k)?;
    }
    let mut latencies = Vec::with_capacity(cfg.queries);
    for _ in 0..cfg.queries {
        let query = random_unit(&mut rng, cfg.dimensions);
        let t = Instant::now();
        index.search(&space, 1, &query, cfg.k)?;
        latencies.push(t.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1000.0);
    }
    let p95_ms = percentile(&latencies, 95.0);
    Ok(VectorScaleReport {
        platform: format!("{}-{}", std::env::consts::OS, std::env::consts::ARCH),
        version: crate::version::VERSION,
        chunks: cfg.chunks,
        dimensions: cfg.dimensions,
        queries: cfg.queries,
        k: cfg.k,
        insert_seconds,
        p50_ms: percentile(&latencies, 50.0),
        p95_ms,
        max_ms: percentile(&latencies, 100.0),
        target_p95_ms: TARGET_P95_MS,
        passed: p95_ms < TARGET_P95_MS,
    })
}
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds an offline ORAG model pack on a connected machine (D-012).
# Usage: scripts/fetch-model-pack.sh <preset> <output-parent-dir>
# Then, on any machine: orag models import <output-parent-dir>/<preset>
set -euo pipefail

preset="${1:-}"
out_parent="${2:-}"
if [ -z "$preset" ] || [ -z "$out_parent" ]; then
  echo "usage: $0 <qwen3-embedding-0.6b-q8_0|qwen3.5-4b-q4_k_m|qwen3-4b-instruct-2507-q4_k_m> <output-dir>" >&2
  exit 1
fi

case "$preset" in
  qwen3-embedding-0.6b-q8_0)
    repo="Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-GGUF"
    revision="370f27d7550e0def9b39c1f16d3fbaa13aa67728"
    file="Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B-Q8_0.gguf"
    sha="06507c7b42688469c4e7298b0a1e16deff06caf291cf0a5b278c308249c3e439"

    role_section=$'[embedding]\ndimensions = 1024\npooling = "last"\nmax_tokens = 1024\nquery_prefix = "Instruct: Given a search query, retrieve relevant passages that answer the query\\nQuery: "\ndocument_prefix = ""\nrequire_trailing_eos = true'
    role="embedding"
    ;;
  qwen3.5-4b-q4_k_m)
    repo="unsloth/Qwen3.5-4B-GGUF"
    revision="e87f176479d0855a907a41277aca2f8ee7a09523"
    file="Qwen3.5-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf"
    sha="00fe7986ff5f6b463e62455821146049db6f9313603938a70800d1fb69ef11a4"
    license_url="https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B/resolve/851bf6e806efd8d0a36b00ddf55e13ccb7b8cd0a/LICENSE"
    role_section=$'[generation]\ncontext_tokens = 8192\nmax_output_tokens = 1024\nprompt_format = "chatml-nothink"'
    role="generation"
    ;;
  qwen3-4b-instruct-2507-q4_k_m)
    repo="unsloth/Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507-GGUF"
    revision="a06e946bb6b655725eafa393f4a9745d460374c9"
    file="Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507-Q4_K_M.gguf"
    sha="3605803b982cb64aead44f6c1b2ae36e3acdb41d8e46c8a94c6533bc4c67e597"

    role_section=$'[generation]\ncontext_tokens = 8192\nmax_output_tokens = 1024\nprompt_format = "chatml"'
    role="generation"
    ;;
  *)
    echo "unknown preset: $preset" >&2
    exit 1
    ;;
esac

sha256() {
  if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null; then sha256sum "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; else shasum -a 256 "$1" | cut -d' ' -f1; fi
}

dest="$out_parent/$preset"
mkdir -p "$dest"
if [ ! -f "$dest/$file" ] || [ "$(sha256 "$dest/$file")" != "$sha" ]; then
  echo "downloading $repo@$revision/$file"
  curl -fL --retry 3 -C - -o "$dest/$file.part" "https://huggingface.co/$repo/resolve/$revision/$file"
  mv "$dest/$file.part" "$dest/$file"
fi
actual=$(sha256 "$dest/$file")
if [ "$actual" != "$sha" ]; then
  echo "checksum mismatch for $file: expected $sha, got $actual" >&2
  exit 1
fi
curl -fsSL --retry 3 -o "$dest/LICENSE" "$license_url"

cat > "$dest/orag-model.toml" <<EOF
id = "$preset"
role = "$role"
file = "$file"
sha256 = "$sha"
license = "Apache-2.0"
license_file = "LICENSE"
source = "https://huggingface.co/$repo"
revision = "$revision"

$role_section
EOF
echo "pack ready: $dest"
```

The SHA-256 values are the LFS object ids that the Hugging Face API reported for these pinned revisions on 2026-10-01. Generation GGUFs come from the `unsloth` quantization repos because Qwen publishes no first-party GGUF for these two models. The license is the base model's Apache-2.0 file at a pinned commit.

- [ ] **Step 2: `crates/orag/tests/real_models.rs`**

```rust
//! Real-model release checks. Run only via scripts/release-model-check.sh.
//! Missing ORAG_MODEL_DIR is a failure, never a silent skip.
#![cfg(feature = "llama")]

use std::ops::ControlFlow;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;

use orag::domain::chunker::ChunkerConfig;
use orag::infer::llama::embedder::LlamaEmbedder;
use orag::infer::llama::generator::LlamaGenerator;
use orag::infer::models::{ModelRole, find_model};
use orag::infer::{Embedder, Generator};
use orag::ingest::format::SourceFormat;
use orag::ingest::worker::{IngestContext, run_once};
use orag::retrieval::answer::{AnswerEngine, AnswerEvent, AnswerSummary};
use orag::retrieval::hybrid::{RetrievalConfig, Retriever};
use orag::store::Store;
use orag::store::documents::NewDocument;
use orag::store::search::SqliteVecIndex;

fn models_dir() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from(std::env::var("ORAG_MODEL_DIR").expect("ORAG_MODEL_DIR must point at installed model packs"))
}

fn embedder() -> LlamaEmbedder {
    LlamaEmbedder::load(&find_model(&models_dir(), "qwen3-embedding-0.6b-q8_0", ModelRole::Embedding).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn generator() -> LlamaGenerator {
    // Same default the product ships with, so the D-007 fallback is tested automatically.
    let id = std::env::var("ORAG_GENERATION_MODEL").unwrap_or_else(|_| orag::config::DEFAULT_GENERATION_MODEL.into());
    LlamaGenerator::load(&find_model(&models_dir(), &id, ModelRole::Generation).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn cosine(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    a.iter().zip(b).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum()
}

#[test]
#[ignore]
fn real_embedding_shape_and_bilingual_similarity() {
    let e = embedder();
    let docs = e
        .embed_documents(&[
            "Ürünler teslim tarihinden itibaren 14 gün içinde iade edilebilir.".into(),
            "Descale the machine every 3 months.".into(),
        ])
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(docs[0].len(), 1024);
    let tr = e.embed_query("İade süresi kaç gündür?").unwrap();
    let en = e.embed_query("What is the return period for products?").unwrap();
    assert!(cosine(&tr, &docs[0]) > cosine(&tr, &docs[1]), "Turkish query must prefer the return policy");
    assert!(cosine(&en, &docs[0]) > cosine(&en, &docs[1]), "cross-lingual query must prefer the return policy");
}

fn answer(question: &str) -> AnswerSummary {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let store = Arc::new(Store::open(&dir.path().join("orag.db")).unwrap());
    let embedder: Arc<dyn Embedder> = Arc::new(embedder());
    let corpus = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("../../eval/corpus/seed/tr-kargo-politikasi.md");
    store
        .enqueue_document(1, NewDocument { filename: Some("tr-kargo-politikasi.md".into()), format: SourceFormat::Markdown, bytes: std::fs::read(corpus).unwrap() })
        .unwrap();
    let ctx = IngestContext { store: store.clone(), embedder: embedder.clone(), chunker: ChunkerConfig::default() };
    while run_once(&ctx).unwrap() {}
    let retriever = Retriever { index: Arc::new(SqliteVecIndex::new(store.clone())), store, embedder, config: RetrievalConfig::default() };
    let engine = AnswerEngine { retriever, generator: Arc::new(generator()) as Arc<dyn Generator> };
    let mut summary = None;
    engine
        .answer(1, question, &mut |event| {
            if let AnswerEvent::Done(done) = event {
                summary = Some(*done);
            }
            ControlFlow::Continue(())
        })
        .unwrap();
    summary.unwrap()
}

#[test]
#[ignore]
fn real_generation_answers_turkish_with_citation() {
    let s = answer("İade süresi kaç gündür?");
    assert!(s.answer.contains("14"), "{}", s.answer);
    assert!(s.citations.contains(&1), "{s:?}");
    assert!(!s.answer.contains("<think>"));
    assert!(!s.abstained);
}

#[test]
#[ignore]
fn real_generation_abstains_when_unsupported() {
    let s = answer("Şirketin kuruluş yılı nedir?");
    assert!(s.abstained, "{}", s.answer);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `scripts/check-binary-deps.sh`** (executable)

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# D-001: the release binary must not depend on non-system dynamic libraries.
set -euo pipefail
bin="${1:-target/release/orag}"
case "$(uname -s)" in
  Darwin)
    deps=$(otool -L "$bin" | tail -n +2 | awk '{print $1}')
    bad=$(echo "$deps" | grep -vE '^(/usr/lib/|/System/Library/)' || true)
    ;;
  Linux)
    if ldd "$bin" | grep -q "not found"; then
      echo "unresolved dynamic libraries:" >&2
      ldd "$bin" | grep "not found" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
    # Resolved path of every dependency; only the base system loader/libc family may appear.
    deps=$(ldd "$bin" | awk '/=>/ {print $3} !/=>/ {print $1}' | grep -v '^$')
    bad=$(echo "$deps" | grep -vE '^(linux-vdso\.so[.0-9]*|/(usr/)?lib(64)?/([^/]+/)?(ld-linux[^/]*|lib(c|m|dl|rt|pthread|gcc_s|stdc\+\+))\.so[.0-9]*)$' || true)
    ;;
  *)
    echo "unsupported OS" >&2
    exit 1
    ;;
esac
if [ -n "$bad" ]; then
  echo "non-system dynamic dependencies:" >&2
  echo "$bad" >&2
  exit 1
fi
echo "binary dependencies ok: $bin"
```

If the Linux check reports `libgomp` or a libstdc++ newer than the oldest supported distribution, build with `llama-cpp-sys-2`'s `static-openmp` and `static-stdcxx` features. Add them as a `release-static` feature of `orag` and use it in release builds. Do not widen the allow-list.

- [ ] **Step 4: `scripts/release-model-check.sh`** (executable)

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Release gate: real models + seed retrieval eval + binary dependency check.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
: "${ORAG_MODEL_DIR:?set ORAG_MODEL_DIR to a directory of installed model packs}"

features=""
if [ "$(uname -s)" = "Darwin" ]; then features="--features metal"; fi

cargo build --release $features
scripts/check-binary-deps.sh target/release/orag
cargo test --release $features --test real_models -- --ignored --test-threads=1

home=$(mktemp -d)
ln -s "$ORAG_MODEL_DIR" "$home/models"
ORAG_HOME="$home" target/release/orag eval retrieval \
  --corpus eval/corpus/seed --dataset eval/datasets/seed.jsonl --out target/release-seed-eval.json
rm -rf "$home"
echo "release model check passed"
```

### Task 23: PDF extraction spike (D-010), report only

**Files:**
- Create: `spikes/pdf-extract/Cargo.toml`, `spikes/pdf-extract/src/main.rs`, `spikes/pdf-extract/corpus.toml`, `spikes/pdf-extract/fetch-corpus.sh`, `.docs/spikes/pdf-extraction.md`
- Modify: root `Cargo.toml` (add `exclude = ["spikes"]` under `[workspace]`)

**Interfaces:** none; the spike produces a decision for the v0.2 plan. Time-box: one working day.

- [ ] **Step 1: Assemble the corpus (12 PDFs)**

`spikes/pdf-extract/corpus.toml` lists each file with `url`, `sha256` and `category`. The PDFs themselves are not committed. One file per category, all publicly downloadable:

1. Turkish official gazette page (Resmî Gazete, multi-column).
2. Turkish university thesis (single column, many headings).
3. Turkish bank or telecom tariff PDF with tables.
4. English two-column academic paper (arXiv, IEEE/ACM template).
5. English single-column government report with tables.
6. Product manual with sidebars and figure captions.
7. PDF with ligatures (`fi`, `fl`) and hyphenation (LaTeX-generated).
8. PDF with a missing ToUnicode map (glyph soup); common in old scanned-then-OCRed government PDFs.
9. Tagged PDF (PDF/UA) with a structure tree.
10. Spreadsheet exported to PDF (wide table).
11. Scanned PDF without a text layer (must be detected as `ocr_required`).
12. Mixed: text pages plus one scanned page.

`fetch-corpus.sh` downloads each URL into `spikes/pdf-extract/corpus/` (gitignored) and verifies the recorded SHA-256.

- [ ] **Step 2: Spike program**

`spikes/pdf-extract/Cargo.toml`:

```toml
[package]
name = "pdf-extract-spike"
version = "0.0.0"
edition = "2024"
publish = false

[dependencies]
anyhow = "1"
pdf_oxide = "0.3"
pdfium-render = "0.9"

[features]
# Static build: PDFIUM_STATIC_LIB_PATH=/path/to/static/lib cargo build --release --features static
static = ["pdfium-render/static"]
```

`spikes/pdf-extract/src/main.rs`:

```rust
//! Extracts every PDF in corpus/ with pdf_oxide and PDFium, writing per-page
//! text to out/<extractor>/<file>/<page>.txt plus timing for manual review.

use std::path::Path;
use std::time::Instant;

use anyhow::Context;

#[cfg(feature = "static")]
fn bind_pdfium() -> anyhow::Result<pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium> {
    let bindings = pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium::bind_to_statically_linked_library()
        .context("static PDFium binding failed")?;
    Ok(pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium::new(bindings))
}

#[cfg(not(feature = "static"))]
fn bind_pdfium() -> anyhow::Result<pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium> {
    let bindings = pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium::bind_to_library(
        pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium::pdfium_platform_library_name_at_path("./lib/"),
    )
    .context("place a PDFium shared library from github.com/bblanchon/pdfium-binaries in ./lib/")?;
    Ok(pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium::new(bindings))
}

fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let pdfium = bind_pdfium()?;
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir("corpus")? {
        let path = entry?.path();
        if path.extension().is_some_and(|e| e == "pdf") {
            run_oxide(&path)?;
            run_pdfium(&pdfium, &path)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn write_page(extractor: &str, pdf: &Path, page: usize, text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let stem = pdf.file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("doc");
    let dir = Path::new("out").join(extractor).join(stem);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)?;
    std::fs::write(dir.join(format!("{page:03}.txt")), text)?;
    Ok(())
}

fn run_oxide(pdf: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let mut doc = pdf_oxide::PdfDocument::open(pdf)?;
    let pages = doc.page_count()?;
    for page in 0..pages {
        // Extraction errors are recorded, never mistaken for empty (scanned) pages.
        let text = match doc.extract_text(page) {
            Ok(text) => text,
            Err(err) => format!("<<EXTRACTION ERROR: {err}>>"),
        };
        write_page("pdf_oxide", pdf, page, &text)?;
    }
    println!("pdf_oxide\t{}\t{pages} pages\t{:?}", pdf.display(), started.elapsed());
    Ok(())
}

fn run_pdfium(pdfium: &pdfium_render::prelude::Pdfium, pdf: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let doc = pdfium.load_pdf_from_file(pdf, None)?;
    for (index, page) in doc.pages().iter().enumerate() {
        let text = match page.text() {
            Ok(t) => t.all(),
            Err(err) => format!("<<EXTRACTION ERROR: {err}>>"),
        };
        write_page("pdfium", pdf, index, &text)?;
    }
    println!("pdfium\t{}\t{} pages\t{:?}", pdf.display(), doc.pages().len(), started.elapsed());
    Ok(())
}
```

If an API name differs in the resolved `pdf_oxide` 0.3.x or `pdfium-render` 0.9.x, fix the call. The spike is throwaway code. The report is the deliverable.

- [ ] **Step 3: Score and write `.docs/spikes/pdf-extraction.md`**

For every PDF and both extractors, score these (pass/fail plus a one-line note):
- reading order;
- column handling;
- table rows kept on one line each;
- Turkish characters intact (`İ ı ş ğ ç ö ü`);
- ligatures expanded;
- garbage detection possible (high ratio of private-use or replacement chars);
- repeated headers/footers identifiable by position;
- scanned page detectable (empty text layer);
- milliseconds per page.

Also record:
- **static PDFium feasibility, proven by a build, not assumed.** Build the spike with `pdfium-render`'s `static` feature against a static PDFium library on macOS arm64 and Linux x86-64. Run `scripts/check-binary-deps.sh` on the result. Record any target where the static build fails as unsupported, with the error. PDFium cannot be recommended for a target where this fails;
- release-binary size delta (`cargo build --release`, compare against the spike without each extractor);
- licenses (PDFium: BSD-3/Apache-2.0; pdf_oxide: check crate metadata);
- the work needed for a static PDFium build on macOS arm64, Linux x86-64 and Windows x86-64.

Decision rule, written into the report:
- Choose the extractor with more passes.
- On a tie, choose `pdf_oxide` (pure Rust, simpler single-binary build).
- If `pdf_oxide` fails Turkish characters or reading order on more than 2 PDFs, choose PDFium **only if its static build passed `check-binary-deps.sh` on every release target**. Otherwise record that neither extractor meets the bar; the v0.2 plan must then resolve it first (e.g. a vendored static PDFium build), and v0.2 cannot start PDF work until it does.
- Either way, list the page-level signals that v0.2 will use for `ocr_required` and `suspicious_layout` warnings.

`scripts/check-benchmark-gate.sh` (executable):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# D-003 release gate: a PASS row at 100k x 1024 for the given version on both reference platforms.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
version="${1:?usage: $0 <version>}"
file=".docs/benchmarks/vector-scale.md"
for platform in macos-aarch64 linux-x86_64; do
  if ! grep -qE "^\| ${platform} \| ${version//./\\.} \| 100000 \| 1024 \|.*\| PASS \|$" "$file"; then
    echo "missing passing 100k/1024 row for ${platform} at ${version} in ${file}" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
done
echo "benchmark gate passed for ${version}"
```

## Roadmap After v0.1 (each milestone gets its own detailed plan)

Version rule (D-017): building milestone M is `0.M.0-alpha.N`; release is `0.M.0`.

### v0.2 — Real documents and model choice

- PDF ingestion with the extractor chosen in Task 23. The Document AST gains `Page`, block bounding boxes and page numbers in citations. Warnings: `ocr_required`, `suspicious_layout`, `no_text`.
- DOCX via a pure-Rust reader, if the spike approach generalizes. HTML via `scraper`-style parsing.
- A 200-question human-checked TR/EN evaluation set (D-018 categories). Answer-level metrics: citation correctness, faithfulness (LLM-as-judge **with the local generator**, sampled human audit), abstention precision/recall.
- **Model decision (D-007 deadline):** compare Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B vs multilingual-e5-large-instruct vs BGE-M3 on that set (recall@10, p95 latency, RSS). The default changes only with evidence.
- Re-index: build a new embedding space in the background and switch the collection atomically. `POST /v1/collections/{id}/reindex`.
- Windows x86-64 CPU build in CI; `check-binary-deps.sh` on all release targets.
- Gate: the seed and 200-question evals do not regress against v0.1; PDF spike corpus passes ≥ 10/12.

### v0.3 — Desktop application (Tauri v2, D-016)

- `apps/desktop` joins the workspace. The `orag` executable is bundled as an `externalBin` sidecar, started with `ORAG_BIND=127.0.0.1:0` and a per-session token. The app reads the `orag listening on` line, supervises the process (restart on crash, kill on exit) and adds its origin to `allowed_origins`.
- Screens: collections, drag-and-drop import with job progress, chat with streamed answers and clickable sources (open document at chunk), model setup. The model downloader runs **only on explicit user action** and verifies SHA-256 against a signed catalog compiled into the app. Offline pack import is also available.
- Installers: macOS (signed, notarized dmg), Windows (MSI with offline WebView2), Linux (AppImage).
- Gate: a non-technical user can go from install to first cited answer offline (with a pack on a USB drive) in under 5 minutes in a scripted usability test.

### v0.4 — Measured quality improvements

- Reranker (Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B on top 20) behind a `Reranker` trait. It ships enabled only if nDCG@10 improves ≥ 3 points at acceptable p95.
- Adaptive routing experiment: deterministic rules vs a small local classifier (encoder head or tev1-0.8B-class model) for "retrieval needed / rerank needed / context sufficient". It ships only if it beats deterministic hybrid on quality or latency on the 200-question set (D-005 revisit).
- Optional query rewriting for multi-document questions, under the same rule.

### v0.5 — Optional OCR build variant (D-011)

- A separate `orag-ocr` executable variant: PaddleOCR-VL GGUF through llama.cpp `mtmd`, plus a layout model. Pages flagged `ocr_required` are routed to it. Gate: OmniDocBench-style subset plus Turkish scanned samples, CPU and Metal latency per page recorded, and a license review of every bundled component.
